Ochoa regained some life with an [Obstinate Baloth](https://web.archive.org/web/20210502212944/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Obstinate+Baloth), but it was forced to trade with the Doppelganger on the following turn when Nelson turned it into a [Vengevine](https://web.archive.org/web/20210502212944/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Vengevine) yet again. Ochoa dropped to eight and was facing two [Vengevine](https://web.archive.org/web/20210502212944/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Vengevine)s. All he had for defense were two [Merfolk Looter](https://web.archive.org/web/20210502212944/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Merfolk+Looter)s and a [Noble Hierarch](https://web.archive.org/web/20210502212944/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Noble+Hierarch). The Hierarch jumped in front of one of the [Vengevine](https://web.archive.org/web/20210502212944/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Vengevine)s on the ensuing attack, but the other went through, dropping Ochoa to three. Nelson finished his turn with an [Obstinate Baloth](https://web.archive.org/web/20210502212944/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Obstinate+Baloth), putting Ochoa up against the wall.

![](https://i.ormos.uk/d7/63/d763576774b8779f545b12119bcb0457ac75bd3d67c99ea8adca3bcb5101a1cb.jpg)*Obstinate Nelson.*

Ochoa had four draws to find an answer on his turn. His first loot put an [Extractor Demon](https://web.archive.org/web/20210502212944/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Extractor+Demon) into his bin. His second sent an [Enclave Cryptologist](https://web.archive.org/web/20210502212944/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Enclave+Cryptologist) there. His final dropped a Doppelganger down. After drawing all of his cards, he played a [Hedron Crab](https://web.archive.org/web/20210502212944/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Hedron+Crab). He followed that up with a [Verdant Catacombs](https://web.archive.org/web/20210502212944/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Verdant+Catacombs), which he immediately sacrificed. With all of the Crab triggers on the stack, Ochoa smiled, "It's time to get big."

The first trigger revealed a temporarily useless [Extractor Demon](https://web.archive.org/web/20210502212944/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Extractor+Demon). His second found him the [Vengevine](https://web.archive.org/web/20210502212944/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Vengevine) he was looking for. With the [Vengevine](https://web.archive.org/web/20210502212944/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Vengevine) now safely in the graveyard, Ochoa played a [Renegade Doppelganger](https://web.archive.org/web/20210502212944/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Renegade+Doppelganger), his second creature of the turn, and the [Vengevine](https://web.archive.org/web/20210502212944/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Vengevine) returned. He now had three blockers to deal with Nelson's three attackers, though only one pair of them would trade. Ochoa made sure to trade his [Vengevine](https://web.archive.org/web/20210502212944/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Vengevine) for Nelson's [Obstinate Baloth](https://web.archive.org/web/20210502212944/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Obstinate+Baloth), the one attacker that doesn't come back, but the two [Vengevine](https://web.archive.org/web/20210502212944/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Vengevine)s were sure to be too much since Ochoa was playing off the top of his deck. After drawing and briefly contemplating activating his Looter, Ochoa conceded.

"Finally won a game on the play, did you?" Ochoa joked.

David Ochoa 1 – Brad Nelson 1

Between games two and three, Nelson went through a few sideboard permutations, most notably considering taking his [Renegade Doppelganger](https://web.archive.org/web/20210502212944/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Renegade+Doppelganger)s out on the draw, but they ultimately found their way back into the deck.

"Alright, next game we swap decks and sideboard for each other," Ochoa suggested. Nelson strongly considered it for a moment or two before politely declining.

Ochoa started the game with a [Verdant Catacombs](https://web.archive.org/web/20210502212944/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Verdant+Catacombs) and a [Noble Hierarch](https://web.archive.org/web/20210502212944/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Noble+Hierarch) that hit play before he had finished grabbing his land from his deck.

"You know, if you get a [Swamp](https://web.archive.org/web/20210502212944/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Swamp), you're going to look kind of stupid," Nelson ribbed.

Nelson followed with an identical open on his turn prompting Ochoa to say, "I love this format." On his turn, Ochoa made a [Merfolk Looter](https://web.archive.org/web/20210502212944/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Merfolk+Looter). Nelson broke the mime game with a different blue two drop on his turn: one of the Doppelgangers he had briefly considered taking out.

With a lot of options, Ochoa took a moment to think. Slowly he turned his [Merfolk Looter](https://web.archive.org/web/20210502212944/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Merfolk+Looter) sideways and inched it forward.

"Attack?" Nelson asked? Ochoa simply nodded. "I'll take it," Nelson said, dropping to sixteen. After combat, Ochoa found an [Obstinate Baloth](https://web.archive.org/web/20210502212944/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Obstinate+Baloth), the first four-powered creature of the match. Nelson had a decision to make on his turn. He had a [Hedron Crab](https://web.archive.org/web/20210502212944/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Hedron+Crab) and a [Vengevine](https://web.archive.org/web/20210502212944/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Vengevine) in his hand, and he needed to decide which engine to get going. He had a Doppelganger in play to double up on whatever he decided. After some thought, he decided to play his land and make a pair of [Vengevine](https://web.archive.org/web/20210502212944/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Vengevine)s. Ochoa thought for a moment before taking the damage, dropping to fourteen.

![](https://i.ormos.uk/34/b1/34b1949b434e7f55f3227b774168dd90c79141bce938ca9bc7efe9831f07a5e8.jpg)

Ochoa used his Looter to put a [Vengevine](https://web.archive.org/web/20210502212944/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Vengevine) into his graveyard on his turn before playing a second [Obstinate Baloth](https://web.archive.org/web/20210502212944/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Obstinate+Baloth). His first swung into the empty space on Nelson's side, dropping him to eleven. On his turn, Nelson got to double up on his options from last turn. He started with a [Hedron Crab](https://web.archive.org/web/20210502212944/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Hedron+Crab), copying it with the Doppelganger. He then played and cracked a [Verdant Catacombs](https://web.archive.org/web/20210502212944/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Verdant+Catacombs), milling himself all of the cards. In the muck, he found absolutely nothing useful. He was really looking for one of the last to [Vengevine](https://web.archive.org/web/20210502212944/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Vengevine)s in his deck since his second creature of the turn, a [Vengevine](https://web.archive.org/web/20210502212944/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Vengevine), would have brought them back, allowing him to do a ton of damage. Instead, he was forced to simply play the [Vengevine](https://web.archive.org/web/20210502212944/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Vengevine) and pass the turn.

On his turn, Ochoa used a nifty trick to return his [Vengevine](https://web.archive.org/web/20210502212944/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Vengevine) to play. He used [Sedraxis Alchemist](https://web.archive.org/web/20210502212944/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Sedraxis+Alchemist) to return itself twice, providing him the two creature casts necessary to trigger the [Vengevine](https://web.archive.org/web/20210502212944/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Vengevine), also leaving him the option to get back any [Vengevine](https://web.archive.org/web/20210502212944/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Vengevine)s that should die at a later time. The near-immortal [Vengevine](https://web.archive.org/web/20210502212944/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Vengevine) attacked in, taking one of Nelson's along with it. For his turn, Nelson took a different approach. He paid six for an [Extractor Demon](https://web.archive.org/web/20210502212944/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Extractor+Demon), allowing his [Renegade Doppelganger](https://web.archive.org/web/20210502212944/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Renegade+Doppelganger) to attack for five in the air, evening the life totals at ten apiece.

Ochoa was in a strange spot. He was facing lethal damage on the board, but he had a [Sedraxis Alchemist](https://web.archive.org/web/20210502212944/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Sedraxis+Alchemist) in his hand. He looted an [Extractor Demon](https://web.archive.org/web/20210502212944/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Extractor+Demon) into his graveyard as the first step. Afterwards, he used the Alchemist to return Nelson's Demon to his hand. His final piece of the puzzle was to play an important [Fauna Shaman](https://web.archive.org/web/20210502212944/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Fauna+Shaman) to trigger and return his [Vengevine](https://web.archive.org/web/20210502212944/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Vengevine). After assembling his board, he sent his two Baloths and the [Vengevine](https://web.archive.org/web/20210502212944/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Vengevine) at Nelson. Nelson traded his [Vengevine](https://web.archive.org/web/20210502212944/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Vengevine) for a Baloth and dropped to two. Nelson drew for his turn and started thinking. He had drawn another creature when what he really needed was a land to try and find an [Extractor Demon](https://web.archive.org/web/20210502212944/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Extractor+Demon). After thinking it through, he shook his head and picked up his cards.

David Ochoa 2 – Brad Nelson 1

Nelson was on the play for the fourth game, which had been important up to this point in the match. Unfortunately, matters were not helped by a double Hierarch, five land hand that he was forced to send back. Ochoa was faced with a more difficult decision about his hand, eventually settling on keeping it. Nelson's second hand was considerably better, containing a [Hedron Crab](https://web.archive.org/web/20210502212944/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Hedron+Crab), [Noble Hierarch](https://web.archive.org/web/20210502212944/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Noble+Hierarch), [Fauna Shaman](https://web.archive.org/web/20210502212944/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Fauna+Shaman), and the lands to cast them all.

Both players started with [Noble Hierarch](https://web.archive.org/web/20210502212944/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Noble+Hierarch)s on the first turn. Nelson chose to use his to attack on the second turn rather than play the Crab he was holding. Players in this matchup have to be careful about the size of their libraries. At any time, their opponent can turn the mill engine over to them, ending the game quite quickly. Instead, he opted for the one point of damage and a [Fauna Shaman](https://web.archive.org/web/20210502212944/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Fauna+Shaman). Ochoa's second-turn play was a [Renegade Doppelganger](https://web.archive.org/web/20210502212944/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Renegade+Doppelganger), his first of the match. As if making up for lost time, he played a second on the following turn. I'm sure he would have rather done something else, but he had yet to draw a second land. It appeared that the reason he was so hesitant about keeping his original seven was that it was a beautiful, but deadly, one land/[Noble Hierarch](https://web.archive.org/web/20210502212944/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Noble+Hierarch) hand.

Nelson kept adding to his board, recruiting an [Obstinate Baloth](https://web.archive.org/web/20210502212944/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Obstinate+Baloth) before attacking with his [Fauna Shaman](https://web.archive.org/web/20210502212944/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Fauna+Shaman). The next turn saw him discard a [Vengevine](https://web.archive.org/web/20210502212944/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Vengevine) to get a [Renegade Doppelganger](https://web.archive.org/web/20210502212944/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Renegade+Doppelganger), which immediately hit play. When he followed with a [Hedron Crab](https://web.archive.org/web/20210502212944/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Hedron+Crab) to bring back his [Vengevine](https://web.archive.org/web/20210502212944/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Vengevine), allowing an attack for twelve, Ochoa conceded.

"Sometimes you roll snake eyes," Ochoa said with a grin.

David Ochoa 2 - Brad Nelson 2

"So…can I get the play?" Nelson sheepishly asked while shuffling.

"I dunno, that's a lot to ask," Ochoa shrugged.

"Aw, come on. Be reasonable!" Nelson pleaded.

"I am being reasonable. More so than most," Ochoa reminded him.

"Yeah, but the winner of this has to play two more matches, and that's a long time to play. I'm trying to be nice so you don't have to go through that," Nelson offered.

"You're so sweet."

Both players kept their opening hand, but only one had a first-turn mana critter. Ochoa's first and second-turn [Birds of Paradise](https://web.archive.org/web/20210502212944/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Birds+of+Paradise) enabled a third-turn [Vengevine](https://web.archive.org/web/20210502212944/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Vengevine), dropping Nelson to sixteen. Nelson worked instead on setting up his hand, going with a [Fauna Shaman](https://web.archive.org/web/20210502212944/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Fauna+Shaman) and [Merfolk Looter](https://web.archive.org/web/20210502212944/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Merfolk+Looter) over the first few turns.

Ochoa got big on his turn, playing a [Hedron Crab](https://web.archive.org/web/20210502212944/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Hedron+Crab) before cracking his [Verdant Catacombs](https://web.archive.org/web/20210502212944/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Verdant+Catacombs). When his first three cards revealed a [Vengevine](https://web.archive.org/web/20210502212944/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Vengevine), Nelson gave the table a light pound and flashing a frown. Ochoa added his second creature, an [Obstinate Baloth](https://web.archive.org/web/20210502212944/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Obstinate+Baloth), to the board and attacked for eight with his two [Vengevine](https://web.archive.org/web/20210502212944/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Vengevine)s. Nelson fell to seven. At the end of Ochoa's turn, Nelson activated his [Fauna Shaman](https://web.archive.org/web/20210502212944/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Fauna+Shaman) to get a [Noble Hierarch](https://web.archive.org/web/20210502212944/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Noble+Hierarch), discarding a [Vengevine](https://web.archive.org/web/20210502212944/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Vengevine). He had a second [Vengevine](https://web.archive.org/web/20210502212944/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Vengevine) in hand, thus was able to some close to matching Ochoa's board. He used his [Merfolk Looter](https://web.archive.org/web/20210502212944/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Merfolk+Looter) to get the second [Vengevine](https://web.archive.org/web/20210502212944/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Vengevine) into his graveyard before playing the two critters to bring both copies back.

![](https://i.ormos.uk/ad/ce/adce219d18fc11f8a69ba1bdf0ad996e207a2a8906ba57e0981c6268d980ee59.jpg)*I've always loved a good opportunity to spread a graveyard.*

Ochoa made an [Extractor Demon](https://web.archive.org/web/20210502212944/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Extractor+Demon) and sent his team. The Baloth and a [Vengevine](https://web.archive.org/web/20210502212944/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Vengevine) ate Nelson's [Vengevine](https://web.archive.org/web/20210502212944/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Vengevine)s, triggering four [Extractor Demon](https://web.archive.org/web/20210502212944/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Extractor+Demon) mills. Hidden within was a third [Vengevine](https://web.archive.org/web/20210502212944/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Vengevine) for Ochoa. Nelson was in trouble. All he could do on his turn was Loot away a land and fetch up a single [Obstinate Baloth](https://web.archive.org/web/20210502212944/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Obstinate+Baloth). He went back up to six, but the [Extractor Demon](https://web.archive.org/web/20210502212944/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Extractor+Demon) was joined by another and one more in the bin after a Demon trigger, and Nelson conceded in the face of a pack of flying Demons.

**David Ochoa 3 – Brad Nelson 2**

## Semifinals – Anthony Eason vs. David Ochoa

*by Monty Ashley*

by Monty Ashley

Eason started the game slowly, with nothing other than [Mountain](https://web.archive.org/web/20210502212944/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Mountain)s on his first two turns. Ochoa, meanwhile had a [Birds of Paradise](https://web.archive.org/web/20210502212944/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Birds+of+Paradise) on his first turn and a [Renegade Doppelganger](https://web.archive.org/web/20210502212944/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Renegade+Doppelganger) on his second, although Eason dealt with the doppelganger with a handy [Lightning Bolt](https://web.archive.org/web/20210502212944/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Lightning+Bolt). When Eason untapped, it was time to start the action as he launched a [Hell's Thunder](https://web.archive.org/web/20210502212944/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Hell%27s+Thunder) into the red zone, taking Ochoa to 16.

On Ochoa's turn, he started out with a [Hedron Crab](https://web.archive.org/web/20210502212944/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Hedron+Crab) and played a [Misty Rainforest](https://web.archive.org/web/20210502212944/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Misty+Rainforest). Immediately, he said "Target me, and in response, crack this," which meant that instead of milling himself for three cards, then getting a land, then milling for another three cards, he was able to get an [Island](https://web.archive.org/web/20210502212944/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Island), shuffle, then mill all six cards at once. He described the cards he milled as "[Extractor Demon](https://web.archive.org/web/20210502212944/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Extractor+Demon) and ... stuff." He added a [Fauna Shaman](https://web.archive.org/web/20210502212944/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Fauna+Shaman) to his army.

Eason [Earthquake](https://web.archive.org/web/20210502212944/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Earthquake)d for two, killing the [Fauna Shaman](https://web.archive.org/web/20210502212944/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Fauna+Shaman) and the [Hedron Crab](https://web.archive.org/web/20210502212944/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Hedron+Crab), but that didn't stop Ochoa from bringing the [Extractor Demon](https://web.archive.org/web/20210502212944/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Extractor+Demon) out from the Graveyard to attack for five. When the smoke cleared, both players were at 13. Ochoa played a [Merfolk Looter](https://web.archive.org/web/20210502212944/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Merfolk+Looter) and passed the turn.

![](https://i.ormos.uk/3a/99/3a997a0eaea18ecd7564b0f85673fcaf34ad89f77de4dc12831d862c0f8496a6.jpg)*Anthony Eason*

Eason sent a [Hell's Thunder](https://web.archive.org/web/20210502212944/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Hell%27s+Thunder) across at Ochoa, taking him to 9. Ochoa responded by attacking with his [Merfolk Looter](https://web.archive.org/web/20210502212944/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Merfolk+Looter) and casting an [Extractor Demon](https://web.archive.org/web/20210502212944/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Extractor+Demon). He was down to one card in hand. When his [Merfolk Looter](https://web.archive.org/web/20210502212944/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Merfolk+Looter) died to [Searing Blaze](https://web.archive.org/web/20210502212944/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Searing+Blaze), the [Extractor Demon](https://web.archive.org/web/20210502212944/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Extractor+Demon) put a [Birds of Paradise](https://web.archive.org/web/20210502212944/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Birds+of+Paradise) and [Enclave Cryptologist](https://web.archive.org/web/20210502212944/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Enclave+Cryptologist) in his graveyard. Almost incidentally, the [Searing Blaze](https://web.archive.org/web/20210502212944/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Searing+Blaze) also put Ochoa at 8.

More relevantly, a [Burst Lightning](https://web.archive.org/web/20210502212944/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Burst+Lightning) on the [Birds of Paradise](https://web.archive.org/web/20210502212944/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Birds+of+Paradise) meant that a [Vengevine](https://web.archive.org/web/20210502212944/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Vengevine) finally milled into Ochoa's graveyard. Combined with the [Extractor Demon](https://web.archive.org/web/20210502212944/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Extractor+Demon), it provided a fighting force of such extraordinary magnitude that Eason's [Ball Lightning](https://web.archive.org/web/20210502212944/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Ball+Lightning) couldn't outrace it.

David Ochoa 1 - Anthony Eason 0

**Game 2**

Eason started strong, with an [Arid Mesa](https://web.archive.org/web/20210502212944/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Arid+Mesa) into a [Mountain](https://web.archive.org/web/20210502212944/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Mountain), followed by a [Goblin Guide](https://web.archive.org/web/20210502212944/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Goblin+Guide) to eat up Ochoa's top two life points. Ochoa also started quickly with a [Noble Hierarch](https://web.archive.org/web/20210502212944/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Noble+Hierarch), [Renegade Doppelganger](https://web.archive.org/web/20210502212944/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Renegade+Doppelganger), and [Birds of Paradise](https://web.archive.org/web/20210502212944/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Birds+of+Paradise) by the end of his second turn. But Eason had a [Forked Bolt](https://web.archive.org/web/20210502212944/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Forked+Bolt) to deal with the [Birds of Paradise](https://web.archive.org/web/20210502212944/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Birds+of+Paradise) and the [Renegade Doppelganger](https://web.archive.org/web/20210502212944/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Renegade+Doppelganger) and his [Goblin Guide](https://web.archive.org/web/20210502212944/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Goblin+Guide) took Ochoa down to 14 life.

Ochoa tried to rebuild with a pair of [Merfolk Looter](https://web.archive.org/web/20210502212944/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Merfolk+Looter)s (and a [Misty Rainforest](https://web.archive.org/web/20210502212944/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Misty+Rainforest) into an [Island](https://web.archive.org/web/20210502212944/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Island), taking himself down to 13), but Eason was now equipped with an [Earthquake](https://web.archive.org/web/20210502212944/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Earthquake) for 1, killing both [Merfolk Looter](https://web.archive.org/web/20210502212944/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Merfolk+Looter)s and the [Noble Hierarch](https://web.archive.org/web/20210502212944/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Noble+Hierarch). And, conveniently for Eason, it took Ochoa down to 12 and Eason to 18. After Eason's [Goblin Guide](https://web.archive.org/web/20210502212944/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Goblin+Guide) got through the combat phase, Ochoa was at 10.

Ochoa was prepared to rebuild, putting out a [Birds of Paradise](https://web.archive.org/web/20210502212944/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Birds+of+Paradise) and a [Renegade Doppelganger](https://web.archive.org/web/20210502212944/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Renegade+Doppelganger), but both fell victim to a [Forked Bolt](https://web.archive.org/web/20210502212944/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Forked+Bolt). Eason's [Goblin Guide](https://web.archive.org/web/20210502212944/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Goblin+Guide) was joined by [Hellspark Elemental](https://web.archive.org/web/20210502212944/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Hellspark+Elemental) to put Ochoa at 5.

Ochoa had only three cards left, and he played all of them: a [Forest](https://web.archive.org/web/20210502212944/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Forest), a [Renegade Doppelganger](https://web.archive.org/web/20210502212944/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Renegade+Doppelganger), and a [Fauna Shaman](https://web.archive.org/web/20210502212944/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Fauna+Shaman). But he didn't have high hopes for the game, asking Eason, ""Am I dead?"

Eason answered, "I got [Earthquake](https://web.archive.org/web/20210502212944/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Earthquake)." That was all Ochoa needed to hear.

David Ochoa 1 - Anthony Eason 1

**Game 3**

Ochoa started with a Mulligan. Eason considered taking one as well, and Ochoa assured him that he could shuffle as long as was necessary.

Ochoa played a [Drowned Catacomb](https://web.archive.org/web/20210502212944/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Drowned+Catacomb) on his first turn and nothing at all on his second. Eason commented, "I feel like I'm gettin' played right now," but that didn't stop him from attacking with a [Hellspark Elemental](https://web.archive.org/web/20210502212944/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Hellspark+Elemental) and taking Ochoa to 17.

![](https://i.ormos.uk/e6/07/e6079ed38b87288cfc431cc52cd5a7d459228db092603499317711c9d8b57647.jpg)*David Ochoa*

Ochoa had a [Hedron Crab](https://web.archive.org/web/20210502212944/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Hedron+Crab) for his third turn, followed by a [Misty Rainforest](https://web.archive.org/web/20210502212944/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Misty+Rainforest), which turned into a [Forest](https://web.archive.org/web/20210502212944/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Forest), put him at 16, and milled six cards into his graveyard. The only notable milled card was an [Extractor Demon](https://web.archive.org/web/20210502212944/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Extractor+Demon). Ochoa added a [Noble Hierarch](https://web.archive.org/web/20210502212944/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Noble+Hierarch) and was done. Eason untapped, unearthed the [Hellspark Elemental](https://web.archive.org/web/20210502212944/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Hellspark+Elemental) (Ochoa down to 13), and killed the [Hedron Crab](https://web.archive.org/web/20210502212944/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Hedron+Crab) with [Burst Lightning](https://web.archive.org/web/20210502212944/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Burst+Lightning).

Ochoa attacked with the [Noble Hierarch](https://web.archive.org/web/20210502212944/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Noble+Hierarch) (putting Eason at 17) and added a [Fauna Shaman](https://web.archive.org/web/20210502212944/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Fauna+Shaman). Eason played [Hell's Thunder](https://web.archive.org/web/20210502212944/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Hell%27s+Thunder), which he powered up with [Teetering Peaks](https://web.archive.org/web/20210502212944/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Teetering+Peaks) before attacking Ochoa down to 7.

Ochoa added a [Merfolk Looter](https://web.archive.org/web/20210502212944/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Merfolk+Looter) to his [Noble Hierarch](https://web.archive.org/web/20210502212944/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Noble+Hierarch) and [Fauna Shaman](https://web.archive.org/web/20210502212944/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Fauna+Shaman) and did not attack. Eason [Earthquake](https://web.archive.org/web/20210502212944/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Earthquake)d for four, which was going to kill all the creatures and also put the life totals at 13 for Eason and 3 for Ochoa. Ochoa used the [Fauna Shaman](https://web.archive.org/web/20210502212944/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Fauna+Shaman) to discard an [Extractor Demon](https://web.archive.org/web/20210502212944/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Extractor+Demon) for a [Birds of Paradise](https://web.archive.org/web/20210502212944/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Birds+of+Paradise), but everything else happened the way Eason wanted it to.

Ochoa had only two permanents: a [Forest](https://web.archive.org/web/20210502212944/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Forest) and a [Drowned Catacomb](https://web.archive.org/web/20210502212944/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Drowned+Catacomb). He played a [Hedron Crab](https://web.archive.org/web/20210502212944/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Hedron+Crab) and a [Birds of Paradise](https://web.archive.org/web/20210502212944/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Birds+of+Paradise), and added a [Forest](https://web.archive.org/web/20210502212944/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Forest), milling an [Island](https://web.archive.org/web/20210502212944/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Island), a [Forest](https://web.archive.org/web/20210502212944/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Forest), and a [Misty Rainforest](https://web.archive.org/web/20210502212944/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Misty+Rainforest). No help there.

Eason used [Smoldering Spires](https://web.archive.org/web/20210502212944/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Smoldering+Spires) to keep the [Birds of Paradise](https://web.archive.org/web/20210502212944/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Birds+of+Paradise) from blocking and sent a [Hell's Thunder](https://web.archive.org/web/20210502212944/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Hell%27s+Thunder) into the red zone. That was enough to finish off Ochoa.

Anthony Eason 2 - David Ochoa 1

![Hell's Thunder](http://gatherer.wizards.com/Handlers/Image.ashx?type=card&name=Hell%27s+Thunder)

![Smoldering Spires](http://gatherer.wizards.com/Handlers/Image.ashx?type=card&name=Smoldering+Spires)

**Game 4**

Ochoa chose to go first and immediately fell behind as he took a pair of mulligans. "The theory behind this is, I'm just trying to make it fair," he explained. "I'm going to go down to my minimum hand size."

With so few cards, Ochoa had his work set out for him. And Eason didn't make it easy on him, starting with a [Goblin Guide](https://web.archive.org/web/20210502212944/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Goblin+Guide) on turn one, a [Hellspark Elemental](https://web.archive.org/web/20210502212944/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Hellspark+Elemental) on turn two, and a [Teetering Peaks](https://web.archive.org/web/20210502212944/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Teetering+Peaks) on turn three (to pump up the [Goblin Guide](https://web.archive.org/web/20210502212944/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Goblin+Guide) while he unearthed the [Hellspark Elemental](https://web.archive.org/web/20210502212944/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Hellspark+Elemental)). Throughout that series, Ochoa had done nothing but crack a [Verdant Catacombs](https://web.archive.org/web/20210502212944/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Verdant+Catacombs), which played right into Eason's hands.

On turn four, at 5 life, Ochoa played a [Vengevine](https://web.archive.org/web/20210502212944/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Vengevine) the old-fashioned way (by paying its mana cost) and asked, "Is that gonna be enough?" Eason shrugged and attacked with his [Goblin Guide](https://web.archive.org/web/20210502212944/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Goblin+Guide) and a [Ball Lightning](https://web.archive.org/web/20210502212944/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Ball+Lightning). There was no block that would keep him alive, and Ochoa conceded.

**Anthony Eason 3 - David Ochoa 1**
