## Semifinals – Josh Utter-Leyton vs. Conrad Kolos

*by Nate Price*

**Game 1**

Utter-Leyton won the die roll and chose to go first. He started his first turn with a [Birds of Paradise](https://web.archive.org/web/20210502212944/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Birds+of+Paradise), followed soon thereafter by a [Lotus Cobra](https://web.archive.org/web/20210502212944/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Lotus+Cobra). Kolos started his own ramp engine with an [Everflowing Chalice](https://web.archive.org/web/20210502212944/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Everflowing+Chalice) for one. Utter-Leyton's draw beyond that was considerably more controlling than aggressive, boasting two [Mana Leak](https://web.archive.org/web/20210502212944/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Mana+Leak)s as the only non-land cards in it. He was forced to rely on his Cobra to bring the beats, so he used a [Sejiri Steppe](https://web.archive.org/web/20210502212944/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Sejiri+Steppe) to force it past the Plant token from Kolos's [Khalni Garden](https://web.archive.org/web/20210502212944/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Khalni+Garden). When Kolos tried to get some more mana with a [Cultivate](https://web.archive.org/web/20210502212944/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Cultivate), Utter-Leyton stopped him with the first Leak.

Utter-Leyton was put into a tough decision on his following turn. He had enough mana to activate and attack with his [Celestial Colonnade](https://web.archive.org/web/20210502212944/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Celestial+Colonnade), but that would leave him unable to cast the second [Mana Leak](https://web.archive.org/web/20210502212944/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Mana+Leak) in his hand. After much thought, he decided that was the correct path and sent his land and Cobra in. Kolos used his Plant token to soak up the two damage from the Cobra, dropping to fourteen. For his turn, all he did was replace it with another [Khalni Garden](https://web.archive.org/web/20210502212944/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Khalni+Garden) and pass the turn with a [Tectonic Edge](https://web.archive.org/web/20210502212944/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Tectonic+Edge) up.

Utter-Leyton thought again about attacking, but was a little wary of the Edge on Kolos's side. He decided it was worth trading lands since he had nothing else to really do and activated his land to attack. [Predict](https://web.archive.org/web/20210502212944/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Predict)ably, Kolos used the [Tectonic Edge](https://web.archive.org/web/20210502212944/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Tectonic+Edge) to kill the Colonnade and blocked the Cobra with his new Plant. Utter-Leyton just passed the turn. As he had done on the previous few turns, Kolos simply played a land and passed it right back.

![](https://i.ormos.uk/08/aa/08aac6b587ba1cef56544744ff6e3d551091e78184fd9ac0cc5a36d308ab5c07.jpg)*All your Utter-Leytons are belong to us.*

It was finally time. Utter-Leyton had been unfortunate enough to draw an [Eldrazi Conscription](https://web.archive.org/web/20210502212944/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Eldrazi+Conscription) a little earlier in the match, but the moment had arrived when he could afford to put it onto his [Lotus Cobra](https://web.archive.org/web/20210502212944/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Lotus+Cobra). [Lotus Cobra](https://web.archive.org/web/20210502212944/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Lotus+Cobra) and a [Verdant Catacombs](https://web.archive.org/web/20210502212944/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Verdant+Catacombs) combined for an extra two mana, and the five lands plus his [Birds of Paradise](https://web.archive.org/web/20210502212944/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Birds+of+Paradise) made up the other six. Utter-Leyton's [Lotus Cobra](https://web.archive.org/web/20210502212944/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Lotus+Cobra) was conscripted into the Eldrazi army. Lumbering over wearing his nifty new pants, the Cobra knocked Kolos to two and ate all but three of his permanents. After a quick read of the Conscription, Kolos conceded.

![](https://i.ormos.uk/8e/32/8e3256fddd0225ae4f04c03b59704fca92062c3a9d6b7a2906272899042964a1.jpg)*Plus what plus what?! Annihiwhat?!*

Josh Utter-Leyton 1 – Conrad Kolos 0

**Game 2**

Kolos started the first game with a Plant token and an [Everflowing Chalice](https://web.archive.org/web/20210502212944/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Everflowing+Chalice). Utter-Leyton looked as if he would just be able to play lands for the first few turns of the game, but he drew a [Verdant Catacombs](https://web.archive.org/web/20210502212944/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Verdant+Catacombs) at the perfect time to enable a second-turn [Lotus Cobra](https://web.archive.org/web/20210502212944/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Lotus+Cobra). This Cobra gave him a couple of options on the following turn. He had a third land drop and a copy of both [Jace, the Mind Sculptor](https://web.archive.org/web/20210502212944/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Jace%2C+the+Mind+Sculptor) and [Elspeth, Knight-Errant](https://web.archive.org/web/20210502212944/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Elspeth%2C+Knight-Errant) in his hand. After much consideration, he decided to put Kolos on a four-turn clock with the Elspeth, lifting his Cobra up for five points of damage. The Jace followed on the next turn and went to work sculpting the perfect hand.

While all of this was going on, Kolos simply continued to accrue lands. After playing his fifth, he tapped out to play a [Primeval Titan](https://web.archive.org/web/20210502212944/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Primeval+Titan). Utter-Leyton had no choice but to allow it. With the Titan's ability, Kolos brought a [Mystifying Maze](https://web.archive.org/web/20210502212944/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Mystifying+Maze) and a second [Eldrazi Temple](https://web.archive.org/web/20210502212944/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Eldrazi+Temple) under his control. Now with plenty of mana and a way to deal with Utter-Leyton's attacker, it looked like he might be able to stabilize long enough to get something massive down.

![](https://i.ormos.uk/ee/7a/ee7ab48b42b843bedba81bc95531b5a29166382d3d64fb154a54d847efd6c56e.jpg)*Don't make Conrad Kolos get Primeval on you!*

Utter-Leyton dug some more with Jace. After loading up on [Mana Leak](https://web.archive.org/web/20210502212944/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Mana+Leak)s, he lifted his Cobra and dropped Kolos to five. A [Knight of the Reliquary](https://web.archive.org/web/20210502212944/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Knight+of+the+Reliquary) came down to play defense, and he passed the turn. Kolos drew his card and sat back to do some math. He picked his Titan up and attacked. With the trigger, he snagged an [Eye of Ugin](https://web.archive.org/web/20210502212944/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Eye+of+Ugin) and a [Tectonic Edge](https://web.archive.org/web/20210502212944/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Tectonic+Edge) from his deck. The Titan ate Jace. After combat, Kolos made a [Relic of Progenitus](https://web.archive.org/web/20210502212944/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Relic+of+Progenitus), which he popped at end of turn to draw a card. Now untapped with all the mana in the world, Kolos could really get to business. First, he attacked with his Titan, fetching up a pair of [Khalni Garden](https://web.archive.org/web/20210502212944/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Khalni+Garden)s. The two extra blockers might prove invaluable over the coming turns. Elspeth dropped to two loyalty. Kolos began meticulously arranging his mana, trying to figure out exactly how it was all going to be used over the next turn. He started with an [Everflowing Chalice](https://web.archive.org/web/20210502212944/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Everflowing+Chalice) for one before passing the turn.

Just as in the first game, Utter-Leyton found himself in a position where he had enough mana to hard cast an [Eldrazi Conscription](https://web.archive.org/web/20210502212944/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Eldrazi+Conscription) on his [Birds of Paradise](https://web.archive.org/web/20210502212944/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Birds+of+Paradise). Combined with a lifted [Lotus Cobra](https://web.archive.org/web/20210502212944/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Lotus+Cobra), Kolos was facing lethal damage, even through his [Mystifying Maze](https://web.archive.org/web/20210502212944/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Mystifying+Maze). After some thought Kolos conceded.

Josh Utter-Leyton 2 – Conrad Kolos 0

**Game 3**

Utter-Leyton seemed to have a knack for winning games in unorthodox fashion, having been reduced to playing his [Eldrazi Conscription](https://web.archive.org/web/20210502212944/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Eldrazi+Conscription) from his hand in both of the previous games. Kolos, on the other hand, had only seen one game in which he really got to get his mana engine going, but he was so far behind at that point that he didn't have an opportunity to stabilize before Utter-Leyton could take the game. He was thrown a bone in the third game, as Utter-Leyton was forced to throw his starting seven back. His second hand was considerably better, leading with a [Noble Hierarch](https://web.archive.org/web/20210502212944/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Noble+Hierarch) on the first turn.

Kolos began with a [Khalni Garden](https://web.archive.org/web/20210502212944/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Khalni+Garden), [Tectonic Edge](https://web.archive.org/web/20210502212944/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Tectonic+Edge), and a [Relic of Progenitus](https://web.archive.org/web/20210502212944/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Relic+of+Progenitus) on his first couple of turns, looking to neuter any [Vengevine](https://web.archive.org/web/20210502212944/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Vengevine) or Knight of the Reliquaries that might appear. Utter-Leyton's good timing on [Lotus Cobra](https://web.archive.org/web/20210502212944/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Lotus+Cobra) continued, as he drew one on the second turn in time to play it. He chose not to play it, however, choosing to feign [Mana Leak](https://web.archive.org/web/20210502212944/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Mana+Leak) instead. Kolos bought the act and passed his third turn with no action. The delay didn't hurt Utter-Leyton at all since a [Misty Rainforest](https://web.archive.org/web/20210502212944/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Misty+Rainforest) combined with his freshly cast [Lotus Cobra](https://web.archive.org/web/20210502212944/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Lotus+Cobra) to power out a [Jace, the Mind Sculptor](https://web.archive.org/web/20210502212944/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Jace%2C+the+Mind+Sculptor) right on time. Kolos took advantage of his new window to resolve some mana acceleration in the form of a [Cultivate](https://web.archive.org/web/20210502212944/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Cultivate).

Jace started picking through Utter-Leyton's deck, finding him the [Mana Leak](https://web.archive.org/web/20210502212944/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Mana+Leak)s he was signaling earlier. Now the hollow threat had some teeth. Joining him was the [Elspeth, Knight-Errant](https://web.archive.org/web/20210502212944/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Elspeth%2C+Knight-Errant) he had in his opener as well. She lifted his [Lotus Cobra](https://web.archive.org/web/20210502212944/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Lotus+Cobra) and sent him into Kolos for six. Kolos simply drew his card and passed the turn. Utter-Leyton activated Jace and had to be pleased to see the smiling face of [Sovereigns of Lost Alara](https://web.archive.org/web/20210502212944/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Sovereigns+of+Lost+Alara) looking back at him. Rather than play them, he simply lifted the Cobra and attacked for six. He preferred to sit on his two [Mana Leak](https://web.archive.org/web/20210502212944/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Mana+Leak)s and stop whatever shenanigans Kolos might try to escape from this situation.

![](https://i.ormos.uk/be/9c/be9c85a4e39a76418644ea3da255ffc2733f17ae36055be0ae11ab3e00f03c9f.jpg)*Utter-Leyton has assembled the machine*

For his turn, Kolos simply played an [Everflowing Chalice](https://web.archive.org/web/20210502212944/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Everflowing+Chalice) for one, and [Eye of Ugin](https://web.archive.org/web/20210502212944/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Eye+of+Ugin), and passed the turn. He was going to need an All is Dust to get himself back into this game. Utter-Leyton kept playing it safe, choosing to simply lift his Cobra and attack for six rather than go for the kill. When Kolos revealed a [Fog](https://web.archive.org/web/20210502212944/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Fog), it seemed his instincts were right. For Kolos, the [Fog](https://web.archive.org/web/20210502212944/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Fog) was amazing. He got the turn he needed to untap and cast his All is Dust, which would dig him out of his hole. Unfortunately, Utter-Leyton had two [Mana Leak](https://web.archive.org/web/20210502212944/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Mana+Leak)s, which was more than Kolos could pay.

**Josh Utter-Leyton 3 – Conrad Kolos 0**

## 3rd Place Playoff:s, and Eldrazi, and Judges. Oh My! – John Kolos vs. David Ochoa

*by Adam Styborski*

**Game 1**

Like in previous games, both competitors maintain their quiet, but forceful, focus. Could the titanic Eldrazi ride up to put Kolos as the third member for the 2010 US National team? Or would the little [Hedron Crab](https://web.archive.org/web/20210502212944/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Hedron+Crab) that could (with some help from [Vengevine](https://web.archive.org/web/20210502212944/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Vengevine)s and [Fauna Shaman](https://web.archive.org/web/20210502212944/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Fauna+Shaman)s too) let Ochoa finish his epic run heading to Chiba, Japan?

With a handshake and "Good luck." exchanged, Ochoa lead with a [Misty Rainforest](https://web.archive.org/web/20210502212944/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Misty+Rainforest) for an [Island](https://web.archive.org/web/20210502212944/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Island), making a [Hedron Crab](https://web.archive.org/web/20210502212944/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Hedron+Crab). Kolos had just a [Forest](https://web.archive.org/web/20210502212944/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Forest).

Ochoa did too, milling nothing exciting away and put a [Fauna Shaman](https://web.archive.org/web/20210502212944/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Fauna+Shaman) down. Kolos cast an [Everflowing Chalice](https://web.archive.org/web/20210502212944/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Everflowing+Chalice) for one counter before passing again. Ochoa paused for a minute before adding another Crab then played a [Drowned Catacomb](https://web.archive.org/web/20210502212944/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Drowned+Catacomb), this time milling into [Extractor Demon](https://web.archive.org/web/20210502212944/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Extractor+Demon).

Kolos had his third [Forest](https://web.archive.org/web/20210502212944/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Forest) and made another Chalice for one counter, moving into a [Cultivate](https://web.archive.org/web/20210502212944/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Cultivate) for a pair of [Forest](https://web.archive.org/web/20210502212944/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Forest)s. Mana was accumulating rapidly. At the end of Kolos's turn, Ochoa used his [Fauna Shaman](https://web.archive.org/web/20210502212944/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Fauna+Shaman) to exchange an [Enclave Cryptologist](https://web.archive.org/web/20210502212944/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Enclave+Cryptologist) for a [Vengevine](https://web.archive.org/web/20210502212944/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Vengevine).

Ochoa added a [Swamp](https://web.archive.org/web/20210502212944/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Swamp) and milled out another 6, this time seeing a [Vengevine](https://web.archive.org/web/20210502212944/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Vengevine). His graveyard was getting quite full of juicy things. His [Extractor Demon](https://web.archive.org/web/20210502212944/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Extractor+Demon) unearthed, coming across to Kolos who fell to 15. Kolos untapped and added a [Forest](https://web.archive.org/web/20210502212944/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Forest), passing back.

Ochoa considered things and chose to [Fauna Shaman](https://web.archive.org/web/20210502212944/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Fauna+Shaman) again, this time dumping the [Vengevine](https://web.archive.org/web/20210502212944/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Vengevine) in his hand and adding one to replace it. For his turn, [Scalding Tarn](https://web.archive.org/web/20210502212944/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Scalding+Tarn) was played and cracked for another [Island](https://web.archive.org/web/20210502212944/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Island), and Ochoa saw two [Extractor Demon](https://web.archive.org/web/20210502212944/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Extractor+Demon)s, and lots of other things, pile into the grave.

![](https://i.ormos.uk/9a/fd/9afdd9778210a1bd8e0c7595557cc08a5e599ee03a341b7885af90650694cc22.jpg)*Ochoa's graveyard was constantly filling up.*

Unearthing one Ochoa sent it across but Kolos had a hard-casted [Summoning Trap](https://web.archive.org/web/20210502212944/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Summoning+Trap) and found an [Ulamog, the Infinite Gyre](https://web.archive.org/web/20210502212944/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Ulamog%2C+the+Infinite+Gyre) that couldn't block the flying Demon. Ochoa could only pass after taking Kolos down to 10.

Kolos untapped with the Eldrazi staring Ochoa down but could he swing with it? The answer was yes, and Ochoa responded to the annihilator trigger by using [Fauna Shaman](https://web.archive.org/web/20210502212944/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Fauna+Shaman) to find a [Renegade Doppelganger](https://web.archive.org/web/20210502212944/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Renegade+Doppelganger). Ochoa threw away all of his creatures and a [Drowned Catacomb](https://web.archive.org/web/20210502212944/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Drowned+Catacomb) before dipping down to 8 life.

Before passing the turn back Kolos checked with a judge: does a [Renegade Doppelganger](https://web.archive.org/web/20210502212944/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Renegade+Doppelganger) get haste if it copies an unearthed [Extractor Demon](https://web.archive.org/web/20210502212944/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Extractor+Demon)? The answer is no since haste is an ability granted by the unearth ability and is not a copy-able characteristic.

"Really?" Ochoa asked. He didn't seem to like how that worked.

Kolos continued his turn by casting a [Terastodon](https://web.archive.org/web/20210502212944/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Terastodon), taking out Ochoa's [Swamp](https://web.archive.org/web/20210502212944/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Swamp) but trading up some of his own lands to add some Elephants to his side. Ochoa cast both [Renegade Doppelganger](https://web.archive.org/web/20210502212944/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Renegade+Doppelganger) and [Noble Hierarch](https://web.archive.org/web/20210502212944/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Noble+Hierarch) to bring back three [Vengevine](https://web.archive.org/web/20210502212944/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Vengevine)s. Ochoa then moved to swing with all three, plus the Doppelganger and Elephant token.

![](https://i.ormos.uk/7d/44/7d446e9a4e9f16e19ad6362e26b25d12dfc6483f7bcfa0784fa1cc9374e7cb46.jpg)*All your Vengevine may not belong to cards named Vengevine.*

Except that Kolos stopped the attack, stating that the trigger on Doppelganger is a may, not must, and Ochoa didn't state the Shapeshifter had now become a [Vengevine](https://web.archive.org/web/20210502212944/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Vengevine). After some discussion, the judges ruled that the time between [Vengevine](https://web.archive.org/web/20210502212944/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Vengevine)s came into play then attacking with every made it clear that Renegade did become a [Vengevine](https://web.archive.org/web/20210502212944/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Vengevine) – ruling by intent of Ochoa.

Kolos wasn't happy about the ruling but agreed and did not appeal. After some discussion the judge encouraged both players to communicate a little clearer and things moved forward again with combat picking right back up: [Terastodon](https://web.archive.org/web/20210502212944/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Terastodon) eating the Doppelganger, and Beasts trading with a Beast and [Vengevine](https://web.archive.org/web/20210502212944/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Vengevine).

Kolos untapped with 2 life and attacked with Ulamog, saying "Annihilator trigger." as he then reached across to the [Terastodon](https://web.archive.org/web/20210502212944/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Terastodon) as well. It was unclear exactly when [Terastodon](https://web.archive.org/web/20210502212944/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Terastodon) was declared to be attacking with respect to the trigger. Ochoa stopped the attack and asked the table judge for his ruling, which was that there was a significant pause between the announcement of the trigger and the [Terastodon](https://web.archive.org/web/20210502212944/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Terastodon) being set as an attacker. Kolos did appeal this ruling, however, and the players were pulled apart for discussions.

Ultimately, the head judge ruled that there was a significant hesitation between the trigger and setting [Terastodon](https://web.archive.org/web/20210502212944/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Terastodon) as an attacker, meaning that only Ulamog was attacking. Ochoa conceded the game with the result.

Ochoa 0 – Kolos 1

**Game 2**

Despite talking the judges, both Kolos and Ochoa were quiet at the table. The sound of shuffling and the murmur of the growing crowd (Josh Utter-Leyton had earned the US Nationals championship already) did little to diminish the atmosphere of challenge between these two.

They did eased a bit for a moment and discussed presenting and counting decks.

"You're the first to count my deck." Ochoa admitted.

"It's something I do." Kolos shared. "Sometimes cards get dropped or fall beneath the table. It's not like I don't have to riffle anyway. Do you ever count decks?"

"No. I don't mind." Ochoa explained.

Ochoa opened with a [Forest](https://web.archive.org/web/20210502212944/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Forest) into a [Noble Hierarch](https://web.archive.org/web/20210502212944/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Noble+Hierarch). Kolos was very similar with [Forest](https://web.archive.org/web/20210502212944/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Forest) into [Joraga Treespeaker](https://web.archive.org/web/20210502212944/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Joraga+Treespeaker). Ochoa sent the Hierarch across for one damage and added and [Island](https://web.archive.org/web/20210502212944/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Island) and [Renegade Doppelganger](https://web.archive.org/web/20210502212944/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Renegade+Doppelganger) to his side.

Adding a counter to the Treespeaker, Kolos played an [Explore](https://web.archive.org/web/20210502212944/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Explore) for a [Khalni Garden](https://web.archive.org/web/20210502212944/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Khalni+Garden) and [Mystifying Maze](https://web.archive.org/web/20210502212944/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Mystifying+Maze). Ochoa had a [Vengevine](https://web.archive.org/web/20210502212944/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Vengevine) and swung in with the [Shapeshifter](https://web.archive.org/web/20210502212944/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Shapeshifter) and [Vengevine](https://web.archive.org/web/20210502212944/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Vengevine), Kolos's Plant token taking a chump block. Kolos then added a [Khalni Garden](https://web.archive.org/web/20210502212944/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Khalni+Garden) and a [Primeval Titan](https://web.archive.org/web/20210502212944/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Primeval+Titan), which found him two [Eldrazi Temple](https://web.archive.org/web/20210502212944/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Eldrazi+Temple)s.

Ochoa played a [Fauna Shaman](https://web.archive.org/web/20210502212944/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Fauna+Shaman) which let him use the Doppelganger to ditch an [Extractor Demon](https://web.archive.org/web/20210502212944/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Extractor+Demon) for another Doppelganger. A [Verdant Catacombs](https://web.archive.org/web/20210502212944/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Verdant+Catacombs) added a [Swamp](https://web.archive.org/web/20210502212944/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Swamp) for him as well. Kolos drew for his turn and carefully surveyed the battlefield and arranged his lands, new with another [Eldrazi Temple](https://web.archive.org/web/20210502212944/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Eldrazi+Temple).

[Primeval Titan](https://web.archive.org/web/20210502212944/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Primeval+Titan) came across, plopping a pair of [Khalni Garden](https://web.archive.org/web/20210502212944/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Khalni+Garden)s for Plant tokens. No blocks from Ochoa dropped him to 13. Passing back, Ochoa unearthed the [Extractor Demon](https://web.archive.org/web/20210502212944/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Extractor+Demon), and the Doppelgangers became Flying Demons. Kolos used his [Mystifying Maze](https://web.archive.org/web/20210502212944/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Mystifying+Maze) away which, thanks for the Demon's triggers, let Ochoa mill himself for four – landing a [Vengevine](https://web.archive.org/web/20210502212944/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Vengevine) and [Extractor Demon](https://web.archive.org/web/20210502212944/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Extractor+Demon).

Kolos dipped to 5 with any blockers for Flying. The exiled Demon have Ochoa two more to the graveyard – revealing a sideboarded [Pithing Needle](https://web.archive.org/web/20210502212944/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Pithing+Needle). Kolos began his turn by counting mana and cast [Emrakul, the Aeons Torn](https://web.archive.org/web/20210502212944/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Emrakul%2C+the+Aeons+Torn).

![](https://i.ormos.uk/4a/83/4a83668f276a81378f33c5421385fc9db91e09f41d495ba1d26a7d1483cc76c8.jpg)*Emrakul, the Gamestate Sundered.*

The Eldrazi drew another scoop from Ochoa.

Ochoa 0 – Kolos 2

**Game 3**

Ochoa was now in the hole two games. He had seen a lot of success in Standard throughout the previous two days but it appeared that matchups had finally caught up to him: Kolos's speed in ramping was driving Eldrazi into play at a blinding pace.

Ochoa chose to play first again, but had to wait for Kolos's mulligan to 6. Hands settled, Ochoa again started off with a [Forest](https://web.archive.org/web/20210502212944/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Forest) for [Noble Hierarch](https://web.archive.org/web/20210502212944/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Noble+Hierarch), as did Kolos with [Forest](https://web.archive.org/web/20210502212944/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Forest) for [Joraga Treespeaker](https://web.archive.org/web/20210502212944/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Joraga+Treespeaker). A [Merfolk Looter](https://web.archive.org/web/20210502212944/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Merfolk+Looter), but missing land drop, was Ochoa's follow up. Kolos, too, missed his land drop but had his own sideboard tech a [Relic of Progenitus](https://web.archive.org/web/20210502212944/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Relic+of+Progenitus) – promising an emptied graveyard for Ochoa.

Ochoa had other plans however and played a [Nature's Claim](https://web.archive.org/web/20210502212944/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Nature%27s+Claim) to remove the obstacle. Using his Looter, he then three away a [Vengevine](https://web.archive.org/web/20210502212944/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Vengevine) and played a [Hedron Crab](https://web.archive.org/web/20210502212944/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Hedron+Crab) but still missed his land drop.

Kolos did have a second, a Mystifying maze, chose to level up his Treespeaker and used the Elf Druid to get an [Everflowing Chalice](https://web.archive.org/web/20210502212944/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Everflowing+Chalice) for one counter. Ochoa drew and tapped his Looter again, sending another [Vengevine](https://web.archive.org/web/20210502212944/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Vengevine) to the graveyard. A [Forest](https://web.archive.org/web/20210502212944/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Forest) came down and Ochoa milled himself, sending an [Extractor Demon](https://web.archive.org/web/20210502212944/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Extractor+Demon), among others, in.

Kolos played a [Forest](https://web.archive.org/web/20210502212944/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Forest) and used his six mana to try set another [Everflowing Chalice](https://web.archive.org/web/20210502212944/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Everflowing+Chalice) into play, this time for three counters. Ochoa responded with a [Unified Will](https://web.archive.org/web/20210502212944/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Unified+Will) to stop the aggressive ramp. Untapping, Ochoa played a [Misty Rainforest](https://web.archive.org/web/20210502212944/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Misty+Rainforest) for an [Island](https://web.archive.org/web/20210502212944/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Island), milling himself more; [Vengevine](https://web.archive.org/web/20210502212944/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Vengevine) was the highlight of the flop.

![](https://i.ormos.uk/06/2e/062e3bcfbfad5ccad15c90900034c25d05cc79bef28e3ad2121baaa4b507b039.jpg)*A very healthy graveyard for Ochoa.*

Using the Looter again, ditching a [Hedron Crab](https://web.archive.org/web/20210502212944/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Hedron+Crab), he went on to play [Renegade Doppelganger](https://web.archive.org/web/20210502212944/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Renegade+Doppelganger) twice over, getting back three [Vengevine](https://web.archive.org/web/20210502212944/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Vengevine)s, and swinging in to bring Kolos down to 7. Kolos's had a sideboarded [Pelakka Wurm](https://web.archive.org/web/20210502212944/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Pelakka+Wurm) which lifted him back to 14.

Ochoa hardcast the fourth [Vengevine](https://web.archive.org/web/20210502212944/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Vengevine) and put a team of six total across.

![](https://i.ormos.uk/b5/36/b5365336a7784c113023f9c3a3f060f014bca4a0cd81097392d5c34da8ddde7c.jpg)*Welcome to beautiful Vengevine City. Current Population: 6.*

Kolos decided to move on to next game.

Ochoa 1 – Kolos 2

**Game 4**

Ochoa took some time considering and reconsidering his sideboarding, but seemed satisfied with what he had done. Kolos finally had his chance to go first after Ochoa shipped his first hand back.

[Eldrazi Temple](https://web.archive.org/web/20210502212944/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Eldrazi+Temple) for Kolos. A tapped [Drowned Catacomb](https://web.archive.org/web/20210502212944/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Drowned+Catacomb) from Ochoa. The [Forest](https://web.archive.org/web/20210502212944/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Forest) from Kolos led to an [Everflowing Chalice](https://web.archive.org/web/20210502212944/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Everflowing+Chalice) for one counter. [Verdant Catacombs](https://web.archive.org/web/20210502212944/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Verdant+Catacombs) joined the drowned variety for Ochoa.

Ramping fast, Kolos had another [Eldrazi Temple](https://web.archive.org/web/20210502212944/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Eldrazi+Temple) and another [Everflowing Chalice](https://web.archive.org/web/20210502212944/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Everflowing+Chalice) for one counter. Ochoa merely doubled up on his [Verdant Catacombs](https://web.archive.org/web/20210502212944/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Verdant+Catacombs). The next turn, however, drew a second [Forest](https://web.archive.org/web/20210502212944/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Forest) and [Primeval Titan](https://web.archive.org/web/20210502212944/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Primeval+Titan) from Kolos, who dug through his deck to put an [Eye of Ugin](https://web.archive.org/web/20210502212944/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Eye+of+Ugin) and [Mystifying Maze](https://web.archive.org/web/20210502212944/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Mystifying+Maze) onto the battlefield.

Ochoa then cracked his Catacombs, bringing out two [Forest](https://web.archive.org/web/20210502212944/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Forest)s. A [Swamp](https://web.archive.org/web/20210502212944/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Swamp) followed by a [Vengevine](https://web.archive.org/web/20210502212944/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Vengevine), held back, was Ochoa's fourth turn. Kolos added a third [Eldrazi Temple](https://web.archive.org/web/20210502212944/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Eldrazi+Temple) to his board before sending the Titan in. The trigger let Kolos set the fourth [Eldrazi Temple](https://web.archive.org/web/20210502212944/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Eldrazi+Temple) and a [Khalni Garden](https://web.archive.org/web/20210502212944/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Khalni+Garden).

Now at 12 life Ochoa sent the [Vengevine](https://web.archive.org/web/20210502212944/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Vengevine) in, but the Plant token took the hit instead. A [Misty Rainforest](https://web.archive.org/web/20210502212944/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Misty+Rainforest) was Ochoa's only play before passing. At the end of the turn Kolos used the Eye to seek out [Emrakul, the Aeons Torn](https://web.archive.org/web/20210502212944/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Emrakul%2C+the+Aeons+Torn).

![](https://i.ormos.uk/97/c1/97c147eb13ce5699f55eec5c3cd9d9c02bc2ce68689306cad5e418088625929d.jpg)*We see what the Eye saw for Kolos.*

This time, with the most powerful Eldrazi looming and the mana to cast it Ochoa packed it in. John Kolos would be heading to Worlds as the third member of the team.

Before stepping away, Kolos shared that through the help of Andy Talaga and Tim Timlin he was able to bring his third place deck; teammates and friends can make all the difference.

Ochoa 1 – Kolos 3

## Finals: You're Hot then You're Cold - Anthony Eason vs. Josh Utter-Leyton

*by Dave Guskin*

Anthony Eason, qualified to this Nationals tournament via Regionals, has launched fire at nearly all of his opponents, and hoped to use that fire to burn through his final opponent, Josh Utter-Leyton, piloting a Mythic Conscription deck. Utter-Leyton needed to use his counter**Magic** and removal over the course of the match to cool Eason's jets and take down the championship.

**Game 1**

![](https://i.ormos.uk/01/f0/01f09d82a48f3df236d341bc90b582a6c1099d39566dff7f368746f2026c8540.jpg)

Utter-Leyton led things off with a Noble Hierarch, which paled in comparative offensive power to the [Goblin Guide](https://web.archive.org/web/20210502212944/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Goblin+Guide) from Eason, putting Utter-Leyton to a quick 18. Utter-Leyton had a turn two [Lotus Cobra](https://web.archive.org/web/20210502212944/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Lotus+Cobra) to temporarily stop the beats, stalling for time and accelerating to his "one card combo" of Sovereigns and single attacker.

However, Eason's [Earthquake](https://web.archive.org/web/20210502212944/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Earthquake) for 1 took out Hierarch and [Lotus Cobra](https://web.archive.org/web/20210502212944/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Lotus+Cobra), leaving the [Goblin Guide](https://web.archive.org/web/20210502212944/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Goblin+Guide) free to attack Utter-Leyton down to 14 after fetchland damage.

Eason dropped a [Smoldering Spires](https://web.archive.org/web/20210502212944/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Smoldering+Spires) to make sure the newly cast Knight couldn't block, then cast [Lightning Bolt](https://web.archive.org/web/20210502212944/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Lightning+Bolt) on the remaining [Lotus Cobra](https://web.archive.org/web/20210502212944/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Lotus+Cobra). He laid down a second [Goblin Guide](https://web.archive.org/web/20210502212944/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Goblin+Guide) to join the first and smashed in, netting Utter-Leyton a [Celestial Colonnade](https://web.archive.org/web/20210502212944/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Celestial+Colonnade) and 4 damage down to 10.

Utter-Leyton just untapped and cast another [Knight of the Reliquary](https://web.archive.org/web/20210502212944/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Knight+of+the+Reliquary), preventing the Goblins from smashing him further. Eason could do nothing but lay a land and pass the turn. [Lotus Cobra](https://web.archive.org/web/20210502212944/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Lotus+Cobra) again joined the team on Utter-Leyton's side of the board.

Eason cast a 6/1 [Ball Lightning](https://web.archive.org/web/20210502212944/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Ball+Lightning), then pumped it to 8/1 and zoomed in. Utter-Leyton blocked with the active Knight and tossed a [Forest](https://web.archive.org/web/20210502212944/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Forest) in the bin to search up a [Sejiri Steppe](https://web.archive.org/web/20210502212944/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Sejiri+Steppe). Protection from red saved the Knight, but 3 damage still trampled over, bringing the Mythic player down to 7 life.

Josh had only a [Birds of Paradise](https://web.archive.org/web/20210502212944/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Birds+of+Paradise) to join its twin on the battlefield, but at least Anthony had nothing to add to his side. That was good news for Josh, who used the two Birds in addition to his four lands to drop [Sovereigns of Lost Alara](https://web.archive.org/web/20210502212944/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Sovereigns+of+Lost+Alara). The 5/5 Knight attacked, became Exalted and then joined the Eldrazi to jump to 16/16 proportions. A single [Goblin Guide](https://web.archive.org/web/20210502212944/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Goblin+Guide) blocked for Eason, but he dropped to 2 life. Another draw step held no miracle for the RDW pilot and he scooped them up.

Eason 0 – 1 Utter-Leyton

Interestingly, Utter-Leyton sided out his Conscription combo for the sideboarded games, along with three [Jace, the Mind Sculptor](https://web.archive.org/web/20210502212944/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Jace%2C+the+Mind+Sculptor), for [Celestial Purge](https://web.archive.org/web/20210502212944/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Celestial+Purge), [Obstinate Baloth](https://web.archive.org/web/20210502212944/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Obstinate+Baloth), [Spell Pierce](https://web.archive.org/web/20210502212944/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Spell+Pierce) and a [Bojuka Bog](https://web.archive.org/web/20210502212944/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Bojuka+Bog). With so much instant-speed burn in his deck, Eason actually had a rougher time with the mid-range monsters than with the exalted combination. For his part, Eason exchanged clunky [Staggershock](https://web.archive.org/web/20210502212944/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Staggershock) and [Burst Lightning](https://web.archive.org/web/20210502212944/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Burst+Lightning) for "elf-killer" [Forked Bolt](https://web.archive.org/web/20210502212944/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Forked+Bolt) and "counter counter" [Ricochet Trap](https://web.archive.org/web/20210502212944/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Ricochet+Trap).

**Game 2**

![](https://i.ormos.uk/3a/33/3a3302413a5b6970ef1c8ace34894e09f7938d186dd83cbe98adbf149827c7d1.jpg)

Eason had his best start – [Mountain](https://web.archive.org/web/20210502212944/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Mountain) into turn one [Goblin Guide](https://web.archive.org/web/20210502212944/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Goblin+Guide) – and after revealing [Spell Pierce](https://web.archive.org/web/20210502212944/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Spell+Pierce) from the top of his deck, Utter-Leyton was down to 18. [Birds of Paradise](https://web.archive.org/web/20210502212944/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Birds+of+Paradise) came down for Utter-Leyton, but his accelerant met a quick demise to [Burst Lightning](https://web.archive.org/web/20210502212944/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Burst+Lightning) and the Teetering Peaks-enhanced Guide took him down to 14.

The [Explore](https://web.archive.org/web/20210502212944/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Explore) and [Celestial Purge](https://web.archive.org/web/20210502212944/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Celestial+Purge) revealed from the top of Josh's deck were welcome sights – one giving Josh more options, the other preventing Anthony from taking all his options from him – but Anthony was too fast, [Ball Lightning](https://web.archive.org/web/20210502212944/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Ball+Lightning) joining the attackers into the red zone. Although the [Purge](https://web.archive.org/web/20210502212944/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Purge) stopped the rampaging Guide, the 6/1 took him to two and the next turn's [Forked Bolt](https://web.archive.org/web/20210502212944/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Forked+Bolt) directly to the dome was too much fire for Josh's hand of threats and [Spell Pierce](https://web.archive.org/web/20210502212944/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Spell+Pierce) against too much untapped land.

Eason 1 – 1 Utter-Leyton

Eason boarded in 2 [Forked Bolt](https://web.archive.org/web/20210502212944/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Forked+Bolt)s and 3 [Ricochet Trap](https://web.archive.org/web/20210502212944/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Ricochet+Trap)s. 4 [Staggershock](https://web.archive.org/web/20210502212944/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Staggershock), 1 [Burst Lightning](https://web.archive.org/web/20210502212944/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Burst+Lightning)

**Game 3**

Josh led with a turn one [Birds of Paradise](https://web.archive.org/web/20210502212944/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Birds+of+Paradise), to which Anthony had the perfect response: turn one [Goblin Guide](https://web.archive.org/web/20210502212944/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Goblin+Guide). Josh took two down to 18, but then cast [Lotus Cobra](https://web.archive.org/web/20210502212944/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Lotus+Cobra) into [Explore](https://web.archive.org/web/20210502212944/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Explore) into [Noble Hierarch](https://web.archive.org/web/20210502212944/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Noble+Hierarch). The Cobra then took one for the team, blocking the [Goblin Guide](https://web.archive.org/web/20210502212944/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Goblin+Guide) after it revealed the powerful [Celestial Purge](https://web.archive.org/web/20210502212944/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Celestial+Purge).

Eason passed the turn after his attack, and Utter-Leyton did the same, merely adding to his lands on the table. The Mythic player knew he was favored if he could just survive long enough. Eason cracked a fetch down to 19, but his [Ball Lightning](https://web.archive.org/web/20210502212944/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Ball+Lightning) was met with a [Mana Leak](https://web.archive.org/web/20210502212944/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Mana+Leak). Utter-Leyton went on the tiniest offensive, bashing with [Noble Hierarch](https://web.archive.org/web/20210502212944/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Noble+Hierarch) and taking Eason down to 18. Eason had nothing – excellent news for Utter-Leyton – and passed it back.

Josh stepped up his offense, activating and attacking with [Celestial Colonnade](https://web.archive.org/web/20210502212944/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Celestial+Colonnade), putting Anthony down to 14. Another swing through the air with the Elemental land put Eason at 10. The RDW player cracked his on-board fetch down to 9, but his hasty [Goblin Guide](https://web.archive.org/web/20210502212944/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Goblin+Guide) was blocked by [Birds of Paradise](https://web.archive.org/web/20210502212944/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Birds+of+Paradise).

The land continued to bash, and with only lands on his side of the board, Eason packed it in.

Eason 1 – 2 Utter-Leyton

**Game 4**

Josh began exactly the opposite of how he would have liked – down two cards from mulligans. Unfortunately for Anthony, he couldn't capitalize on that fact, with only a tapped [Teetering Peaks](https://web.archive.org/web/20210502212944/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Teetering+Peaks) for his first turn. Josh laid down a fetch into [Forest](https://web.archive.org/web/20210502212944/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Forest) and a [Birds of Paradise](https://web.archive.org/web/20210502212944/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Birds+of+Paradise).

Eason did have a [Searing Blaze](https://web.archive.org/web/20210502212944/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Searing+Blaze) to handle the accelerant, taking Utter-Leyton down to 16. The process repeated the next turn – especially problematic because in addition to going down 13, he still had only the single [Forest](https://web.archive.org/web/20210502212944/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Forest).

![Birds of Paradise](http://gatherer.wizards.com/Handlers/Image.ashx?type=card&name=Birds+of+Paradise)

![Searing Blaze](http://gatherer.wizards.com/Handlers/Image.ashx?type=card&name=Searing+Blaze)

Utter-Leyton did use his land to good effect, summoning first a [Noble Hierarch](https://web.archive.org/web/20210502212944/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Noble+Hierarch) and then another and a third Birds. However, Eason had an [Earthquake](https://web.archive.org/web/20210502212944/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Earthquake) for 4, taking the life totals to 14-9 in his favor. Utter-Leyton didn't have any plays off of his two mana, and [Smoldering Spires](https://web.archive.org/web/20210502212944/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Smoldering+Spires) made sure the untapped [Birds of Paradise](https://web.archive.org/web/20210502212944/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Birds+of+Paradise) couldn't block. Although the Mythic player did have a [Celestial Purge](https://web.archive.org/web/20210502212944/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Celestial+Purge) for the incoming [Hellspark Elemental](https://web.archive.org/web/20210502212944/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Hellspark+Elemental), the also-incoming [Goblin Guide](https://web.archive.org/web/20210502212944/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Goblin+Guide) took him to 7.

Josh needed to stop the burning, and soon. He had a [Lotus Cobra](https://web.archive.org/web/20210502212944/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Lotus+Cobra) off the top to trade with the Guide, which gave him some breathing room. When Anthony had no additional pressure, Josh was able to land an Elspeth, and suddenly Anthony's life total didn't seem so far off.

One turn later, with no answer to Elspeth's +1 [Angelic Blessing](https://web.archive.org/web/20210502212944/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Angelic+Blessing), Eason extended the hand and Josh Utter-Leyton became the 2010 U.S. National Champion!

**Eason 1 – 3 Utter-Leyton**

![](https://i.ormos.uk/92/b2/92b2d2d453b29150700b967b1a1b3b2ced7b0498f310152ad366c86943948994.jpg) *Josh Utter-Leyton*
